Concept v2 — Claude, 2026-09-26
• Rear wheel drive, 1 motor per wheel, 7:1 in one stage:
   pinion 10T m0.6 → 70T (Ø42) on the inside of the wheel, centre distance 24
• Front: steered by servo GWS Mini STD, C-hub knuckles on M3 kingpins, ±30°, servo upside down, TPU tyres
• Axles fixed (Ø5 steel), wheels on 2× MR105 each → 8 of the 10 bearings
• Motor in a cradle in the body, clamp on top with 2× M2 into inserts
• Battery 3×18650 in front of the rear axle, PCB above it on M3 standoffs
• PCB gets buck converter 12 V → 5 V + servo header; powerbank goes
• Car 212 × 144 × 61 mm, wheelbase 150, rear track 124 ^clV2txt1

			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": [],
			"updated": 1790375005463,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "Concept v2 — Claude, 2026-09-26\n• Rear wheel drive, 1 motor per wheel, 7:1 in one stage:\n   pinion 10T m0.6 → 70T (Ø42) on the inside of the wheel, centre distance 24\n• Front: steered by servo GWS Mini STD, C-hub knuckles on M3 kingpins, ±30°, servo upside down, TPU tyres\n• Axles fixed (Ø5 steel), wheels on 2× MR105 each → 8 of the 10 bearings\n• Motor in a cradle in the body, clamp on top with 2× M2 into inserts\n• Battery 3×18650 in front of the rear axle, PCB above it on M3 standoffs\n• PCB gets buck converter 12 V → 5 V + servo header; powerbank goes\n• Car 212 × 144 × 61 mm, wheelbase 150, rear track 124",
			"rawText": "Concept v2 — Claude, 2026-09-26\n• Rear wheel drive, 1 motor per wheel, 7:1 in one stage:\n   pinion 10T m0.6 → 70T (Ø42) on the inside of the wheel, centre distance 24\n• Front: steered by servo GWS Mini STD, C-hub knuckles on M3 kingpins, ±30°, servo upside down, TPU tyres\n• Axles fixed (Ø5 steel), wheels on 2× MR105 each → 8 of the 10 bearings\n• Motor in a cradle in the body, clamp on top with 2× M2 into inserts\n• Battery 3×18650 in front of the rear axle, PCB above it on M3 standoffs\n• PCB gets buck converter 12 V → 5 V + servo header; powerbank goes\n• Car 212 × 144 × 61 mm, wheelbase 150, rear track 124",
			"fontSize": 36,
			"fontFamily": 5,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "Concept v2 — Claude, 2026-09-26\n• Rear wheel drive, 1 motor per wheel, 7:1 in one stage:\n   pinion 10T m0.6 → 70T (Ø42) on the inside of the wheel, centre distance 24\n• Front: steered by servo GWS Mini STD, C-hub knuckles on M3 kingpins, ±30°, servo upside down, TPU tyres\n• Axles fixed (Ø5 steel), wheels on 2× MR105 each → 8 of the 10 bearings\n• Motor in a cradle in the body, clamp on top with 2× M2 into inserts\n• Battery 3×18650 in front of the rear axle, PCB above it on M3 standoffs\n• PCB gets buck converter 12 V → 5 V + servo header; powerbank goes\n• Car 212 × 144 × 61 mm, wheelbase 150, rear track 124",

			"lineHeight": 1.25,
			"labelPosition": null,
			"hasTextLink": false,
			"link": null
